Model before rewriting:

find x: int(5..20)
find y: int(0..5)
find z: int(0..6)

such that

!((x / (y / z)) = 10)

--

x
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
x
new variables:
  find x#offset_1: bool
  find x#offset_2: bool
  find x#offset_3: bool
  find x#offset_4: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(x#offset_4),or([!(x#offset_3),or([!(x#offset_2),or([!(x#offset_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])
  or([and([SATInt(Offset, [x#offset_1,x#offset_2,x#offset_3,x#offset_4;int(1..)] [5, 20]) >= 5,SATInt(Offset, [x#offset_1,x#offset_2,x#offset_3,x#offset_4;int(1..)] [5, 20]) <= 20;int(1..)]);int(1..)])

--

SATInt(Offset, [x#offset_1,x#offset_2,x#offset_3,x#offset_4;int(1..)] [5, 20]) >= 5
   ~~> select_integer_relation ([("SAT", 18500)])
__0
new variables:
  findAux __0: bool
new SAT encoding decisions:
  define(__0 <-> integer-weighted(1*x#offset_1, 2*x#offset_2, 4*x#offset_3, 8*x#offset_4) GreaterEqual 0) using None with [PbTermGroup { start: 0, end: 4, structure: BoundedBinary { lower: 0, upper: 15 } }]

--

SATInt(Offset, [x#offset_1,x#offset_2,x#offset_3,x#offset_4;int(1..)] [5, 20]) <= 20
   ~~> select_integer_relation ([("SAT", 18500)])
__1

true,
